# Graph Report - thingtime  (2026-11-14)

## Corpus Check
- 5859 files · ~12,727,979 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.
- Unclassified: 117 file(s) not represented in the graph (top: (none) 35, .css 18, .entitlements 11)

## Summary
- 79071 nodes · 226860 edges · 2060 communities (1398 shown, 662 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 95% EXTRACTED · 5% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 10210 edges (avg confidence: 0.87)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `60fef9a8`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
